- Rian predicts the Garden cargo route will fail at Anchor Crown; the first sensor loss instead occurs inside the Transit Belt receiving loop.

## E226 — 첫 서비스 실패

CONTEXT STATUS: **FULL — SOURCE-BOUND DEEP EXECUTION PACK**
CONTEXT READINESS: **READY**
Source Card: [[ga3-e219-226-episode-cards-v1]]
Date: CY 742-11-04.
POV / information source: Transit receiving operator, Garden tug master and trial board through current feeds.
HIGH_WATCH_BAND: `N/A`

### Common six-field contract

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_MAIN**  
complete the first full Garden–Transit transfer under the new trial.

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_SECONDARY**  
stopping on every unknown can make the service too slow to prevent spoilage and shortages.

**PHYSICAL_ANCHOR / WORK-SYSTEM CARRIER**
- [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Movement** — six cargo pods/three tugs/approximately 186 t enter the planned transfer chain; current pilot identities and cold-chain states recognized. 2. **Unknown branch** — Transit receiving loop loses direct state on one branch; physical temperature remains inside range and sponsor/market actors push continuation. 3. **Valid stop** — local receiving operator stops two pods and redirects one through manual mutual-aid handling; no automatic protagonist override. 4. **Mixed result** — three pods distribute on time, two delayed, one pod’s sensitive contents suffer approximately 18% downgrade/spoilage; one Transit section receives less than planned while another avoids cold-loop rupture. Decisive choice: preserve physical/service safety and disclose unequal delivery rather than declare the trial successful or total failure … [source continues]

**STATE_CHANGE**
- sequence, identity and physical receiving must be separated/redesigned; the 30-day trial remains conditional.
- no major injury or loop rupture; current failure mode is measured and local workaround identified.
- real current-state evidence and a workable redesign direction.

**COST_OR_REFUSAL**
- approximately 14–18 t equivalent service/value loss or downgrade; unequal distribution, claims and public criticism.
- food/biological loss, creditor/pay conflict, labor/data burden and no citywide throughput gain.

**REENTRY_ANCHOR**

- [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Order board** — current requests are separated into immediate life service, time-bounded prevention, strategic restoration and ordinary/creditor production. 2. **Household reality** — worker representatives expose unpaid care, transport and injury time; Haren’s first model undercounts workers absent because of dependent care. 3. **Essential band** — the strike agrees to hospital cold-chain couplings, Garden pump seals, current fire/pressure parts and Luma’s injury-support fabrication; Spine B blanks receive a diagnostic-only quota. 4. **Refusal** — defense reserve and creditor export parts are delayed despite political pressure; one hospital request is reduced because its inventory report double-counted reserved stock. Decisive choice: workers accept a capped essential shift with rotating consent, paid hours and independent technical stop, not unlimited emergency duty … [source continues]

## E250 — 도시를 고치지만 도시에 없는 사람들

CONTEXT STATUS: **FULL — SOURCE-BOUND DEEP EXECUTION PACK**
CONTEXT READINESS: **READY**
Source Card: [[ga3-e248-254-episode-cards-v1]]
Date: CY 742-12-01.
POV / information source: Savo, Transit utility workers, Surface/Anchor service managers and Haren.
HIGH_WATCH_BAND: `N/A`

### Common six-field contract

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_MAIN**  
create legal work and emergency-response standing for workers whose current records do not grant ordinary district access.

**ACTIVE_DESIRE_SECONDARY**  
service access can become coerced labor: the city may recognize people only while they keep dangerous systems running.

**PHYSICAL_ANCHOR / WORK-SYSTEM CARRIER**
- [APPROVED SECTION CARRIER EVIDENCE] [Scene units] 1. **Current roster** — 280 workers across water, pressure, fire, sanitation and cable response; 91 have provisional service standing, 63 have conflicting records and 126 rely on shift-specific passes. 2. **After-shift trap** — one pressure worker cannot access family housing after a schedule change; another can enter a fire zone but not the adjacent clinic. 3. **Terms** — service residency must include wages, injury claims, ordinary emergency/medical access and the right to stop/leave work; dependents are not labor collateral. 4. **Pilot expansion** — 280 workers and 412 named dependents/household members enter a 30-day service-residency cohort with appeal and independent welfare review. Decisive choice: recognize workers and defined households as current residents for services, not merely as labor credentials … [source continues]

**STATE_CHANGE**
